- **brainstorming**: Activar SIEMPRE antes de implementar cualquier feature, refactor, o cambio no trivial. Diseño-primero: valida el problema real, explora alternativas, documenta decisiones arquitectónicas, identifica riesgos, y produce un DESIGN-&lt;feature&gt;.md antes de tocar código. Fuerza pensamiento estratégico: no es "¿cómo lo codifico?" sino "¿debería codificarlo así?". Impide implementación apresurada. Cubre: alcance, límites de módulo, contratos de datos, fallback paths, observabilidad, seguridad, escalabilidad. Resultado: plan claro, riesgos visibles, decisiones reversibles. - **brainstorming**: 需求澄清与方案批准 Skill：命中真实需求未决或用户明确要求时，逐项收敛目标、用户价值、非目标、约束、成功标准和方案取舍，并取得用户明确批准。 触发条件（满足任一）： 1. 用户希望澄清新功能或行为变更的真实需求（如“先把需求想清楚”“我们到底要解决什么问题”） 2. 请求中的目标、价值、边界或成功标准会实质影响决策但尚不明确（例如“做一个企业级登录，直接开工”） 3. 用户希望比较决策级方案并批准方向（如“给我几个选择并推荐一个”“这个功能还值得继续吗”） 不触发（留给 spec-driven-workflow、openspec、diagnosing-bugs、tdd 或对应执行 Skill）： - 已有可定位的 Brainstorming 批准记录，只要求写规格、技术设计或任务，留给 openspec - 目标明确的故障诊断、已有批准规格的实现/测试，或明确、机械、低风险且不改变用户可观察行为的小改动 默认路由：存在真实需求未决或用户明确要求澄清时进入本 Skill；需求决策已明确且有可定位证据时可直接交给 OpenSpec。只有经仓库证据确认明确、机械、低风险、易回滚且不改变用户可观察行为、公共契约、数据、权限或架构时才走快速路径。 ([Vitus213/specpatrol](https://github.com/Vitus213/specpatrol/tree/HEAD/src/specpatrol/skillpack/skills/brainstorming))
- **brainstorming**: Targeted clarification and concise user communication for ambiguous or high-impact work. ([vkaylee/leedevkit](https://github.com/vkaylee/leedevkit/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/brainstorming))
- **brainstorming**: Use when a user brings a new feature, design question, or architectural decision that needs exploration before code. Guides Socratic discovery of requirements, proposes multiple approaches, and produces an approved spec under docs/ before any implementation begins. ([vonschau/symbiopowers](https://github.com/vonschau/symbiopowers/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorming))
- **brainstorming**: Explore intent, requirements, and design options before committing to a plan, surfacing unknowns and trade-offs early. Use when the Router routes an ambiguous or STANDARD/EPIC request at its start, before the planning skill. ([Vtquan123/strix](https://github.com/Vtquan123/strix/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorming))
- **brainstorming**: 在任何创意工作之前必须使用 - 创建功能、构建组件、添加功能或修改行为。在实施之前探索用户意图、需求和设计。合并了 discuss-requirements 和 describe-interaction 的功能。 ([walker-hzx/AI-Assistant](https://github.com/walker-hzx/AI-Assistant/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorming))
- **brainstorming**: Use before any creative work — creating features, building components, adding functionality, or modifying behavior — OR when revising an existing feature design. Triggers on: 'create feature X', 'build X', 'add functionality', 'update feature X', 'revise the design', 'change the plan for Y', 'modify the existing design'. Explores intent and design (new features) or focused change delta (revisions) before implementation. - **brainstorming**: convierte ideas en diseños aprobados paso a paso mediante diálogo colaborativo antes de escribir código. ([williebs64uwu-create/OFFSZN](https://github.com/williebs64uwu-create/OFFSZN/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/planning/brainstorming))
